Update options:
A) Projected gradient:
```
def _step_project(a,g,n,lr):
    g = g - np.mean(g)  # keep sum
    scale = np.max(np.abs(g))
    a = a - lr * g / scale
    a = np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum(); if s<...; return a/s*n
```
But center by mean not exactly same as sum preservation after clipping. Could improve by using `g_centered = g - (np.sum(a*g)/np.sum(a))` to enforce first-order sum preservation. Use gradient of objective with Lagrange sum; subtract constant μ=(∑a_i g_i)/(∑a_i) so ∇F - μ1. Since sum fixed. Then update.

B) Log-space:
```
def _log_update(a,g,n,lr):
    S=a.sum()
    mu = np.sum(a*g)/S
    gu = a*(g-mu)
    # gu sum? sum gu = sum a(g-mu)=0
    scale = np.max(np.abs(gu)) or rms
    # update log(a): loga += -lr*gu/scale
    loga = np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)) - lr*gu/scale
    # center loga to avoid overflow: subtract max
    loga = loga - np.max(loga)
    e=np.exp(loga); a=e/e.sum()*n
```
But if a has zeros, log -inf; set floor. This can be stable. However gradient gu for a_i near zero maybe small; values can become exponentially small. Good.

Which is better? Projected can set zeros and use boundary; log may stay positive and maybe smoother. We can alternate or choose.

Use momentum in log or a. Let's implement a unified `smooth_run` with `mode` parameter (0 projected, 1 log). It will compute c and p, gradient, update, anneal tau, adapt step.

Pseudo:
```
def _smooth_run(a, n, deadline, tag, tau_frac=0.5, lr=0.05, log_mode=1):
    L=_fft_len(n)
    a=a.copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0); a=a/a.sum()*n
    best=a; best_o=_score(a,n,L)
    tau=max(1.0,n*tau_frac)
    mom=np.zeros(n); beta=0.9
    lr_cur=lr
    stall=0; iter=0
    while time < deadline:
       c,A=_conv(a,L)
       mc=np.max(c[:2n-1]); o=2*mc/n
       if o<best_o-1e-10: update best, stall=0 else stall++
       p=_softmax(c_valid,tau)
       p_pad=zeros(L); p_pad[:2n-1]=p
       g=_grad_fft(a,L,A,p_pad)
       # center with a-weighted mean
       S=a.sum(); mu=np.sum(a*g)/S
       # projected gradient direction:
       if log_mode:
           dir = a*(g-mu)
       else:
           dir = g - mu  # because sum preserved if μ=mean? Actually Sμ=S mean a? Need μ constant not weighted? For constraint 1^T a=0, subtract (1/S)∑g_i? The Lagrange multiplier is (∑g_i)/S independent of a for linear constraint. But to minimize F with sum fixed, gradient projected onto tangent: g - mean(g). But if use log, different. For projected, use g - np.mean(g). For log, gu = a(g - (∑a g)/S).
       # scale direction
       mg=max(abs(dir)); if mg<1e-14 break
       v = beta*v - lr_cur*dir/mg
       if log_mode:
           loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); loga += v  # v is update to log, centered? v sum not zero? dir sum? a*(g-mu) sum=0, v sum approx0. Good.
           loga = loga - np.max(loga)
           a=np.exp(loga); a*=n/a.sum()
       else:
           a = a + v
           a=np.maximum(a,0); a*=n/a.sum()
       # adapt lr if objective worsens too much? We can't know yet. Could backtracking.
       tau *= 0.99995; lower bound n*0.001
       stall handling reduce lr or reset momentum
       iter++
    return best,best_o
```
But using momentum in log with v scaled by dir/mg each iter; if mg changes, okay. `v` update maybe too large. Need choose lr ~0.1 for log? For projected, lr ~0.1.

Need backtracking to avoid divergence:
After updating a, compute new objective (c) before next iteration? We already compute c next. Could check if new_o > old_o*(1+tol) then roll back and reduce lr. To avoid extra conv, we can compute c next and adjust. Implement within loop: keep `last_o`. After update, next iteration compute c_new. If c_new > last_o + 0.1*last_o + 1e-12, rollback to a_previous, halve lr, maybe clear momentum. But we need a_previous. We can do:
- At top compute current o. If current_o > trial_o_accept_threshold, rollback. But structure messy.
Simplify: use small lr and anneal; no rollback. But robust.

Could implement line search per step? Expensive but manageable if few iterations. For high dimension, not.

Use exact hard descent:
```
def _hard_run(a,n,deadline,tag):
   L=...; best...
   lr=0.05; mom zeros; beta=0.85
   for:
      c,A = conv
      k=argmax c_valid; mc=c[k]; o=...
      # gradient direct:
      g=zeros; js=max(0,k-n+1); je=min(n-1,k)+1; idx=np.arange(js,je); g[idx]=2*a[k-idx]
      mu=np.mean(g) (or weighted?)
      dir=g-mu
      mg=maxabs
      v=beta*v - lr*dir/mg
      a = a + v; clip normalize
      if bad rollback reduce lr
      every N reduce lr
```
For hard, gradient of max coefficient only. If multiple maxima, choose one; may oscillate. Could use top-K active set: choose K indices with largest c, compute p uniform or softmax over top K, gradient via FFT or direct sum. This smooths over ties.
`_active_set_k(c, K)` returns p with softmax over top K maybe tau small. Then use FFT grad. This is like smooth with hard tau but restricted. Could improve.

Maybe use "pool adjacent violators"? The c coefficients are quadratics; at optimum, the maximum set likely large? Previous active set LP. We can solve local KKT: Given support of active indices H, find a minimizing ∑_{k∈H} c_k / |H| (or softmax). This is linear? The weighted sum of c_k is a quadratic form a^T B a. Minimizing an indefinite quadratic over simplex. But gradient of average active = (1/|H|)∑_{k∈H} ∇c_k = direct. Use gradient descent on it. Good.

Natural adaptation: At each step, determine active set as top K c coefficients where c > threshold*max. Use weights proportional to exp((c_k-max)/tau) but maybe restrict top K to reduce bias. ```

`_conv(a, L):`
```

A_k = ∑_j a_j e^{-2πi jk/L}. P_k=∑_i p_i e^{-2πi i k/L}. Product P_k conj(A_k)=∑_{j,i} p_i a_j e^{-2πi (i-j) k/L}. Inverse ifft index m: (1/L)∑ e^{2πi m k/L} product = ∑ p_i a_j δ_{m, i-j mod L} = ∑_i p_i a_{i-m mod L} (where a index = i-m) = ∑_j p_{j+m} a_j. Yes if j = i-m, i=j+m. So output m = ∑_j p_{j+m} a_j. Good. For m=0..n-1, desired. Good. Multiplying by 2. Great.

`_softmax_weights(c, tau):`
```
c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
mx = np.max(c)
e = np.exp((c-mx)/tau)
s = e.sum()
if s < 1e-300: return np.full(c.shape, 1.0/c.size)
return e/s
```
For very small tau, e may underflow except max; fine (onehot).

`_smooth_run` parameters: `tau0_frac`, `tau_min_frac`, `lr0`, `lr_max`, `use_log`, `momentum`, `it_limit`, `tag`.
Implement robust with line search but no separate objective after top? We'll restructure:
```
def _smooth_run(a,n,deadline,tag,tau0_frac=1.0,tau_min_frac=1e-4,lr0=0.05,log_mode=1,momentum=0.9,it_limit=...):
    S=n
    a=_normalize(a,S)
    L=_fft_len(n)
    c,A=_conv(a,L)
    best=a.copy(); best_o=_score(c,n)
    tau=max(1.0,n*tau0_frac)
    lr=lr0
    v=np.zeros(n)
    beta=momentum
    stall=0; it=0
    last_improve = best_o
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it+=1
       mc=float(np.max(c)); o=2*mc/n
       if o < best_o - 1e-10: best_o=o; best=a.copy(); stall=0
       else: stall+=1
       # weights
       p=_softmax_weights(c,tau)
       # optional flat q? no
       g=_grad_from_weights(a,L,A,p)
       # direction
       if log_mode:
           Scur=a.sum(); mu=np.sum(a*g)/Scur
           dir=a*(g-mu)
       else:
           dir=g - g.mean()
       scale=float(np.max(np.abs(dir)))
       if scale < 1e-16:
           # no direction, perturb or break
           if stall>1000: break
           continue
       v = beta*v - lr*dir/scale
       # limit step to avoid huge
       vmax=np.max(np.abs(v)); if vmax>0.5: v*=0.5/vmax
       a_new=...
       c_new,A_new=_conv(a_new,L); o_new=2*max(c_new)/n
       if np.isfinite(o_new) and o_new <= o + max(1e-10, 0.02*o):
           # accept
           a=a_new; c=c_new; A=A_new
           if o_new < best_o - 1e-10: ...
           lr = min(lr*1.05, 0.3)
           stall maybe reset if accepted? no
       else:
           # reject, reduce
           v *= 0.25  # or zero
           lr *= 0.5
           if lr < 1e-6: break
       # anneal tau based on stall or iteration
       if stall > 2000:
           if tau > n*tau_min_frac: tau = max(n*tau_min_frac, tau*0.8)
           else: # add noise
               a=_normalize(a*(1+1e-3*rng_normal), n)
               v.fill(0)
           stall=0
       # periodic reduce momentum? 
       if it % 10000 ==0: beta=max(0.5,beta*0.999)
```
But this computes c_new even if accept, next loop uses c. Good. However if reject, we computed c_new wasted. Fine.

Tolerance 0.02*o may accept large increases; better for initial flatness? Maybe 0.005. Use `tol = 1e-8 + 0.01*o`.

Line search with momentum may accept increases; if too many, adjust. Could instead always compute step without momentum and if reject reduce. Simpler and reliable. Momentum can cause loops. Maybe use small beta=0.5 or 0.0. But acceleration helpful. Use beta=0.6.

For log mode, `a_new` from loga + v. Since v is scaled by lr*dir/scale and momentum, v elements O(lr). If lr=0.1, v up to0.3 maybe. Softmax changes values factor e^0.3=1.35. Good. To avoid change too large, clip v to [-0.5,0.5]. In code above. For projected, v up to 0.3 absolute, values ~1, okay.

def _hard_run(a,n,deadline,tag,lr0=0.05,log_mode=0,momentum=0.5):
   S=n; L=...

   for ...:
      c,A=conv (if not have)
      mc=max; k=argmax; o=...
      # weights: top K coefficients sorted? Could use p = softmax(c, tau= mc*0.005)
      p=_softmax_weights(c, max(1e-6, mc*0.005))  # near top
      # Or if top multiple close: p uniform over topK where c > mc - 0.005*mc
      g=grad_fft
      dir...
      step, line search
```

- Template interpolation: a = (1-λ)a + λ template.
Return normalized.

Detailed helper definitions:

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _fft_len(n):
    need = 2*int(n)-1
    L=1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    return L
```

`_normalize(a, S):`
```
def _normalize(a,S):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return np.full_like(a, S/float(a.size))
    return (a/s)*S
```
If S maybe n. Good.

`_convolve_fft(a, L):`
```
def _convolve_fft(a, L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=L)
    out_len=2*a.size-1
    c=c[:out_len]
    # real already
    return c,A
```
Small negative values: For max, `np.max(c)` may be negative if all zeros? But a positive, max positive. For weights, exp of negative okay. Could set `c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)` to avoid negative p? But if small negative due roundoff at zeros, okay. It changes objective tiny. We can do `c = np.where(c > 0.0, c, 0.0)`. However if all clipped, sum? not important. For gradient, c not used except weights. Good.

`_grad_fft(a, L, A, w):`
```
def _grad_fft(a, L, A, w):
    n=a.size
    wp=np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    wl=w.size
    wp[:wl]=w
    W=np.fft.rfft(wp)
    g=np.fft.irfft(W*np.conj(A), n=L)[:n]
    return 2.0*g
```
Check if `w` length differs (2n-1). For flat gradient, w length same. Good.

`_softmax_weights(c, tau):` as above. Ensure finite.

`_top_weights(c, gap_frac):`
```
def _top_weights(c, gap_frac=0.01):
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if mx <=0: return normalized uniform
    mask = c >= mx - gap_frac*mx
    cnt=int(mask.sum())
    p=np.zeros_like(c)
    p[mask]=1.0/cnt
    return p, mx
```

`_score_from_c(c,n):`
```
m=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
return 2.0*m/float(n)
```

`_internal_obj(a,n):`
```
a=_normalize(a,float(n))
L=_fft_len(n); c,A=_convolve_fft(a,L); return _score_from_c(c,n), a
```
But this re-normalizes and conv. For candidate scoring.

Template generator:
```
def _generate_initial(kind, n, rng, parent=None):
    if n <=0: return [1.0]
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
    x = i/(n-1) if n>1 else np.zeros(n)
    x = np.clip(x, 1e-9, 1-1e-9) # for powers
    if kind == 9 or (kind == 10 and parent is not None):
        if parent is not None:
           p=np.asarray(parent,float).ravel()
           p=np.clip(p,0,None)
           if p.size != n:
              xs=np.linspace(0,1,p.size); xn=np.linspace(0,1,n)
              t=np.interp(xn,xs,p)
           else: t=p.copy()
           if t.sum()<1e-300: t=np.ones(n)
           return _normalize(t,float(n))
    # else modern kinds
    if kind==0: t=np.ones(n)
    elif kind==1: t=1.0 + np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)  # ramps
    elif kind==2:
        A=float(rng.uniform(-0.85,0.85)); t=1.0 + A*np.cos(np.pi*x)
    elif kind==3:
        A=float(rng.uniform(-0.85,0.85)); t=1.0 + A*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    elif kind==4:
        A=float(rng.uniform(-0.8,0.8)); B=float(rng.uniform(-0.5,0.5)); t=1.0 + A*np.cos(np.pi*x) + B*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
    elif kind==5:
        p=float(rng.choice([0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0,1.5,2.0])); q=float(rng.choice([0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0,1.5,2.0])); t=np.power(x,p)*np.power(1.0-x,q)
    elif kind==6:
        c=float(rng.uniform(0.25,0.75)); w=float(rng.uniform(0.08,0.3)); t=np.exp(-0.5*((x-c)/w)**2)
    elif kind==7:
        f=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.complex128); f[0]=1.0
        # random low frequencies
        for k in (1,2,3,4,-1,-2,-3,-4):
            kk = n + k if k<0 else k? rfft? Simpler use full fft? We can use real time sinusoidal:
        # Use sum of random sinusoids:
        t=1.0
        for _ in range(3):
            freq=int(rng.integers(1,5)); phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)); amp=float(rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)); t += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*x + phase)
        t=np.abs(t)+0.05
    elif kind==8:
        t=rng.random(n)+1e-4
    else:
        t=np.ones(n)
    t=np.ascontiguousarray(t,dtype=np.float64)
    t=np.where(np.isfinite(t), t, 1.0)
    t=np.maximum(t,0.0)
    if t.sum()<1e-12:
       t=np.ones(n)
    return _normalize(t,float(n))
```
Note kind 7 with multiple sinusoids may produce negative; abs+0.05. Good.

Maybe include kind 10 for uniform random smooth via FFT. Simpler enough.

def _smooth_run(a, n, deadline, tag, tau0_frac=0.5, tau_min_frac=1e-4, lr0=0.1, log_mode=True, momentum=0.5, anneal_s=60.0, flat_alpha=0.0, use_flat=False, it_limit=1000000):

while time.monotonic() < deadline:
    it +=1

        best_o=o; best=a.copy(); stall=0

    if use_flat:
       T=1.0/(2*n-1)
       q = c - T
       qnorm = np.linalg.norm(q)
       if qnorm > 1e-12: q = q/qnorm * 2.0
       gf = _grad_fft(a,L,A,q)
       g = g + flat_alpha*gf
    if log_mode:
       Scur=float(a.sum()); mu=np.sum(a*g)/Scur
       dir=a*(g-mu)
    else:
       dir=g - g.mean()
    scale=max(abs(dir))
    if scale<1e-15:

       a = _perturb_block(a,n,rng?) But smooth doesn't have rng. Could return.

       continue

    if maxv > 0.4: v *= 0.4/maxv

               break

def _perturb_sequence(a,n,rng,sigma,mode):
    b=a.copy(); S=b.sum()
    if mode==0:
       w=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//4)))
       s=int(rng.integers(0,n-w))
       b[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,size=w))
    elif mode==1:
       f1=rng.uniform(1,4); f2=rng.uniform(4,12); ph1=rng.uniform(0,2π); ph2=...
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
       b *= np.exp(sigma*(0.6*np.sin(2π f1 x+ph1)+0.4*np.sin(2π f2 x+ph2)))
    elif mode==2:
       idx1=rng.integers(n); idx2=...; swap blocks?
       # random block swap to explore positions
       w=min(n//50, n//2); w=max(2,w); s1,s2 distinct
       b[[slice(s1,s1+w), slice(s2,s2+w)]] = ... swap
    b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s<...; return b/s*S
```

       o=o2
    return a,o,n

```
def _best_trig_init(n, rng, parent=None):
    x=np.arange(n)/(n-1)

Now, about `np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=L)`: For real `A` from rfft, square is not conjugate, so inverse gives circular linear convolution with time-reversal? Wait if A(z)=∑ a_i z^i, product A(z)^2 corresponds to convolution. DFT of product is A_k^2. Inverse DFT yields circular convolution of a with a. Yes. `irfft(A*A)` okay. For `A*A`, the input to irfft is Hermitian symmetric? Since A_k complex not necessarily conjugate pair squared? For real a, A_{L-k}=conj(A_k). Squared: A_{L-k}^2=conj(A_k)^2 = conj(A_k^2). So Hermitian. Good.

For crosscorr `P*conj(A)`: P from p real, P_{L-k}=conj(P_k). conj(A_{L-k})? A_{L-k}=conj(A_k); conj(A_{L-k})=A_k. Product P_{L-k}conj(A_{L-k})=conj(P_k) A_k = conj(P_k conj(A_k))? conj(P_k conj(A_k)) = conj(P_k) A_k. Hermitian. Good.

Potential issue: `np.fft.irfft` expects first point real; small imaginary discarded. Good.

Optimizes nonnegative sequence minimizing 2n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)^2.

Helper `_next_power_of_two(L):`
```
def _fft_len(n):
    need = 2*int(n)-1
    L=1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    if L < 16: L=16
    return L
```

`_normalize` as above. Ensure output dtype float64 and shape.

`_convolve_fft`:
```
def _convolve_fft(a, L):
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A*A, n=L)
    length = 2*a.size - 1
    c = c[:length]
    # small negatives
    if c.size:
       c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, A
```
`np.maximum` copies? okay. If negative due roundoff only. Could positive values tiny negative at edges? a nonnegative so true c nonnegative; okay.

`_score_from_c`:
```
def _score_from_c(c, n):
    if c.size == 0: return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return 2.0*m/float(n)
```

`_template_score` maybe.

`_softmax_weights`: Need handle `c` maybe integer? convert? no.
```
def _softmax_weights(c, tau):
    c=np.asarray(c,dtype=np.float64)
    if c.size==0: return c
    tau=float(tau)
    if tau < 1e-12:
       p=np.zeros_like(c); imax=int(np.argmax(c)); p[imax]=1.0; return p
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return np.full(c.shape,1.0/c.size)
    e=np.exp((c-mx)/tau)
    s=float(e.sum())
    if s<1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.full(c.shape,1.0/c.size)
    return e/s
```

`_top_weights`:
```
def _top_weights(c, gap_frac):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx<=0: return np.full(c.shape,1/c.size),mx
    gap=max(1e-9, gap_frac)*mx
    mask = c >= mx - gap
    cnt=int(mask.sum())
    if cnt<=0: p=zeros; p[argmax]=1
    else: p=mask.astype(float)/cnt
    return p,mx
```

   if scale < 1e-15:

      continue

def _perturb_sequence(a,n,rng,mode=0,sigma=0.2):
   n=int(n); b=a.copy(); S=float(a.sum())
   if n<2: return b
   if mode==0:
      w=int(rng.integers(2, max(3, n//20)))
      s=int(rng.integers(0, n-w))
      b[s:s+w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=w))
   elif mode==1:
      x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
      f1=float(rng.uniform(1,4)); f2=float(rng.uniform(4,12)); p1=...; p2=...
      mod = 0.7*np.sin(2π f1 x + p1) + 0.3*np.sin(2π f2 x + p2)
      b *= np.exp(sigma*mod)
   elif mode==2:
      w=max(2,n//50); s1=int(rng.integers(0,n-w)); s2=int(rng.integers(0,n-w)); if s1==s2: s2=(s1+w)%n? ensure
      # swap blocks if no overlap
      if abs(s1-s2) < w: s2=(s1+w)%n
      tmp=b[s1:s1+w].copy(); b[s1:s1+w]=b[s2:s2+w]; b[s2:s2+w]=tmp
   b=np.maximum(b,0); s=b.sum(); if s<...; return b/s*S
```

if s2 == s1: s2 = (s1 + max(1,w//2)) % max(1,n-w)
```

`_quick_refine`:

Detailed `_smooth_run` code:
```
def _smooth_run(a, n, deadline, tag, rng, tau0_frac=0.6, tau_min_frac=1e-5, lr0=0.12, lr_max=0.25, log_mode=True, momentum=0.5, anneal_s=60.0, flat_alpha=0.0):
    n = int(n)
    S = float(n)
    deadline = float(deadline)
    a = _normalize(a, S)
    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
       c,A=_convolve_fft(a,_fft_len(n)); return a, _score_from_c(c,n)
    L=_fft_len(n)
    c,A=_convolve_fft(a,L)
    best=a.copy(); best_o=_score_from_c(c,n)
    start=time.monotonic()
    tau0=max(1.0, n*tau0_frac); tau_min=max(1e-4, n*tau_min_frac)
    lr=float(lr0); beta=float(momentum); it=0; stall=0
    v=np.zeros(n)
    print(f"smooth {tag} start obj={best_o:.6f} n={n} log={log_mode}")
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       it += 1
       old_o = _score_from_c(c,n)
       if old_o < best_o - 1e-10:
           best_o=old_o; best=a.copy(); stall=0
           if it % 20000 == 1:
               print(f"  smooth {tag} it={it} obj={best_o:.6f} tau={tau:.3f} lr={lr:.5f}")
       else:
           stall += 1
       p=_softmax_weights(c,tau)
       g=_grad_fft(a,L,A,p)
       if flat_alpha != 0.0:
           T=1.0/(2.0*float(n)-1.0)
           q=c - T
           qn=np.linalg.norm(q)
           if qn > 1e-12:
              gf=_grad_fft(a,L,A,q/(qn*0.5)?)
```
Need scale q. If normalized by norm *? `q/qn` has norm 1; gradients maybe O(1/sqrt(n))? Actually q values O(n), norm ~n, q/qn O(1/√n)? gradient maybe O(1). To make comparable to g (O(1)), use `q * (2.0 / (np.linalg.norm(q) + 1e-12))` gives norm 2. Good.
```
              gf = _grad_fft(a,L,A, q * (2.0/(qn+1e-12)))
              g = g + flat_alpha*gf
```
Then direction.
```
       if log_mode:
          Scur=float(a.sum())
          mu = np.sum(a*g)/Scur if Scur>0 else 0
          d = a*(g-mu)
       else:
          d = g - np.mean(g)
       scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
       if scale < 1e-16:
          if stall > 1000: break
          # perturb tiny
          a=_normalize(a*(1+1e-6*rng.normal(0,1,n)),S); c,A=_convolve_fft(a,L); v.fill(0); stall=0; continue
       v = beta*v - lr*d/scale
       maxv = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
       if maxv > 0.35:
           v *= 0.35/maxv
       if log_mode:
           loga=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))
           loga = loga - np.max(loga)
           loga = loga + v
           loga = loga - np.max(loga)
           e=np.exp(loga)
           s=float(np.sum(e))
           if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
               a = np.full(n, S/n)
           else:
               a = e * (S/s)
       else:
           a_new = a + v
           a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
           s=float(np.sum(a_new))
           if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
               a=np.full(n,S/n)
           else:
               a=a_new*(S/s)
       c_new,A_new=_convolve_fft(a,L)
       o_new=_score_from_c(c_new,n)
       tol=1e-10 + 0.005*old_o
       if np.isfinite(o_new) and o_new <= old_o + tol:
           c=c_new; A=A_new
           if o_new < old_o - 1e-10:
               lr = min(lr*1.05, lr_max)
           else:
               lr = max(lr*0.98, lr0*0.4)
       else:
           v = v*0.2
           lr = max(lr*0.5, 1e-7)
           beta = max(0.0, beta*0.95)
       elapsed = max(1e-6, time.monotonic()-start)
       tau = max(tau_min, tau0 * float(np.exp(-elapsed / max(1.0, anneal_s))))
       if stall > 4000:
           w = 1.0 + 0.02*np.abs(rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n))
           a = _normalize(a*w, S)
           c,A = _convolve_fft(a,L)
           v.fill(0.0)
           beta = max(0.0, beta*0.5)
           lr = max(lr, lr0*0.3)
           stall = 0
    # last objective
    c,A=_convolve_fft(best,L)? To get best_o? best_o already. But best_a sum maybe. return best,best_o
```

    end1=time.monotonic() + 0.65*rem_end

def _normalize(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64) * S
    np.fill(a[~np.isfinite(a)], 0.0)

       return np.full(a.shape, S / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a *= S / s
    return a

    best = a.copy()
    stall = 0
    if time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:

        last_print = time.monotonic()
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2
for nonnegative sequences a.

Approach:
- Fix sum(a) internally to n for numerical stability.
- Use FFT-based convolution gradients.
- Scout good low-complexity templates (trigonometric, beta-like, random smooth).
- Refine with softened-max gradient descent in log-space, then exact active-set
  descent in projected space.
- Use perturb/refine loops to escape local minima and maintain all required
  top-level helper functions.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _fft_len(n):
    need = 2 * int(n) - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    if L < 16:
        L = 16
    return L


def _normalize(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64) * float(S)
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, float(S) / float(a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    a *= float(S) / s
    return a


def _convolve_fft(a, L):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)
    length = 2 * a.size - 1
    c = c[:length]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c, A


def _grad_fft(a, L, A, w):
    n = a.size
    wp = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    wp[:w.size] = w
    W = np.fft.rfft(wp)
    g = np.fft.irfft(W * np.conj(A), n=L)[:n]
    return 2.0 * g


def _score_from_c(c, n):
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * m / float(n)


def _softmax_weights(c, tau):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return c
    tau = float(tau)
    if tau <= 1e-12:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return p
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    e = np.exp((c - mx) / tau)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _top_weights(c, gap_frac):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    n = c.size
    if n == 0:
        return c, 0.0
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64), mx
    gap = max(1e-12, float(gap_frac)) * mx
    mask = c >= (mx - gap)
    cnt = int(mask.sum())
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if cnt <= 0:
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p[mask] = 1.0 / cnt
    return p, mx


def _score_normalized_template(t, n, L):
    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64)
    a = _normalize(t, float(n))
    c, _ = _convolve_fft(a, L)
    o = _score_from_c(c, n)
    if not np.isfinite(o):
        o = np.inf
    return o, a


def _generate_initial(kind, n, rng, parent=None):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)

    if kind == 9 and parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if p.size == 1:
                t = np.full(n, float(p[0]), dtype=np.float64)
            elif p.size != n:
                xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
                xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                t = np.interp(xn, xs, p)
            else:
                t = p.copy()
            if t.sum() <= 1e-12:
                t = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            t = t + 1e-6 * rng.random(n)
            return _normalize(t, float(n))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xc = np.clip(x, 1e-12, 1.0 - 1e-12)

    if kind == 0:
        t = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == 1:
        t = np.linspace(0.2, 1.8, n)
    elif kind == 2:
        A = float(rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85))
        t = 1.0 + A * np.cos(np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 3:
        A = float(rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85))
        t = 1.0 + A * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 4:
        A = float(rng.uniform(-0.80, 0.80))
        B = float(rng.uniform(-0.50, 0.50))
        t = 1.0 + A * np.cos(np.pi * x) + B * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 5:
        p = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0])))
        q = float(rng.choice(np.array([0.2, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0])))
        t = np.power(xc, p) * np.power(1.0 - xc, q)
    elif kind == 6:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.30))
        t = np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c) / w) ** 2)
    elif kind == 7:
        t = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for _ in range(3):
            freq = int(rng.integers(1, 6))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.60))
            t = t + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + ph)
        t = np.abs(t) + 0.05
    elif kind == 8:
        t = rng.random(n) + 1e-4
    else:
        t = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    t = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64)
    t = np.where(np.isfinite(t), t, 1.0)
    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
    if t.sum() <= 1e-12:
        t = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = t + 1e-6 * rng.random(n)
    return _normalize(t, float(n))


def _best_parameterized_init(n, rng, parent=None):
    n = int(n)
    L = _fft_len(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    xc = np.clip(x, 1e-12, 1.0 - 1e-12)

    cos1 = np.cos(np.pi * x)
    cos2 = np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    cos3 = np.cos(3.0 * np.pi * x)

    best_a = None
    best_o = np.inf

    def consider(t):
        nonlocal best_a, best_o
        tt = np.asarray(t, dtype=np.float64) + 1e-6 * rng.random(n)
        o, a = _score_normalized_template(tt, n, L)
        if o < best_o and np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
            best_o = o
            best_a = a

    if parent is not None:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size > 0:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if p.size == 1:
                consider(np.full(n, float(p[0])))
            elif p.size != n:
                xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
                xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                consider(np.interp(xn, xs, p))
            else:
                consider(p)

    for t in (
        np.ones(n),
        np.linspace(0.2, 1.8, n),
        np.linspace(1.8, 0.2, n),
        np.linspace(0.01, 1.0, n),
        np.linspace(1.0, 0.01, n),
    ):
        consider(t)

    vals1 = np.array([-0.9, -0.6, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9])
    vals2 = np.array([-0.6, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.6])
    vals3 = np.array([-0.4, -0.2, 0.0, 0.2, 0.4])

    for a1 in vals1:
        base1 = 1.0 + float(a1) * cos1
        for a2 in vals2:
            base2 = base1 + float(a2) * cos2
            for a3 in vals3:
                t = base2 + float(a3) * cos3
                if t.min() < 0.0:
                    t = np.maximum(t, 0.0)
                consider(t)

    powers = np.array([0.3, 0.5, 0.8, 1.2, 2.0])
    for p in powers:
        for q in powers:
            consider(np.power(xc, float(p)) * np.power(1.0 - xc, float(q)))

    for _ in range(6):
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
        w = float(rng.uniform(0.08, 0.25))
        consider(np.exp(-0.5 * ((x - c) / w) ** 2))

    for _ in range(6):
        t = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for __ in range(3):
            freq = int(rng.integers(1, 7))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.70))
            t = t + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + ph)
        consider(np.abs(t) + 0.05)

    for _ in range(4):
        consider(rng.random(n) + 1e-3)

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _generate_initial(0, n, rng, parent)
        best_o = _score_from_c(_convolve_fft(best_a, L)[0], n)

    return best_a, float(best_o)


def _smooth_run(a, n, deadline, tag, rng, tau0_frac=0.6, tau_min_frac=1e-5,
                lr0=0.12, lr_max=0.25, log_mode=True, momentum=0.5,
                anneal_s=60.0, flat_alpha=0.0):
    n = int(n)
    S = float(n)
    a = _normalize(a, S)
    L = _fft_len(n)
    c, A = _convolve_fft(a, L)
    best = a.copy()
    best_o = _score_from_c(c, n)

    if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        return best, best_o

    start = time.monotonic()
    last_print = start
    tau0 = max(1.0, n * float(tau0_frac))
    tau_min = max(1e-4, n * float(tau_min_frac))
    tau = tau0
    lr = float(lr0)
    beta = float(momentum)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    print(f"smooth {tag} n={n} start={best_o:.6f} log={bool(log_mode)}")

    while time.monotonic() < min(float(deadline), start + 10**9):
        it += 1
        old_o = _score_from_c(c, n)

        if old_o < best_o - 1e-10:
            best_o = old_o
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            if time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:
                print(f"  smooth {tag} it={it} obj={best_o:.6f} "
                      f"tau={tau:.3f} lr={lr:.5f}")
                last_print = time.monotonic()
        else:
            stall += 1

        p = _softmax_weights(c, tau)
        g = _grad_fft(a, L, A, p)

        if flat_alpha != 0.0:
            T = 1.0 / (2.0 * float(n) - 1.0)
            q = c - T
            qn = float(np.linalg.norm(q))
            if qn > 1e-12:
                gf = _grad_fft(a, L, A, q * (2.0 / (qn + 1e-12)))
                g = g + float(flat_alpha) * gf

        if log_mode:
            Scur = float(a.sum())
            if Scur <= 1e-300:
                Scur = float(n)
            mu = float(np.sum(a * g) / Scur)
            d = a * (g - mu)
        else:
            d = g - float(np.mean(g))

        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if scale < 1e-15:
            if stall > 1000:
                break
            a = _normalize(a * (1.0 + 1e-6 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)), S)
            c, A = _convolve_fft(a, L)
            v.fill(0.0)
            stall = 0
            continue

        v = beta * v - lr * (d / scale)
        maxv = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
        if maxv > 0.35:
            v *= 0.35 / maxv

        if log_mode:
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
            loga = loga + v
            loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
            e = np.exp(loga)
            s = float(e.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                a = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                a = e * (S / s)
        else:
            a_new = a + v
            a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
            s = float(a_new.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                a = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                a = a_new * (S / s)

        c_new, A_new = _convolve_fft(a, L)
        o_new = _score_from_c(c_new, n)
        tol = 1e-10 + 0.005 * old_o

        if np.isfinite(o_new) and o_new <= old_o + tol:
            c = c_new
            A = A_new
            if o_new < old_o - 1e-10:
                lr = min(lr * 1.05, lr_max)
            else:
                lr = max(lr * 0.98, lr0 * 0.4)
        else:
            v = v * 0.2
            lr = max(lr * 0.5, 1e-7)
            beta = max(0.0, beta * 0.95)

        elapsed = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - start)
        tau = max(tau_min, tau0 * float(np.exp(-elapsed / max(1.0, float(anneal_s)))))

        if stall > 4000:
            w = 1.0 + 0.02 * np.abs(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n))
            a = _normalize(a * w, S)
            c, A = _convolve_fft(a, L)
            v.fill(0.0)
            beta = max(0.0, beta * 0.5)
            lr = max(lr, lr0 * 0.3)
            stall = 0

    return best, float(best_o)


def _hard_run(a, n, deadline, tag, rng, lr0=0.08, lr_max=0.20,
              log_mode=False, momentum=0.4, gap0=0.05, gap_min=1e-5,
              anneal_s=60.0):
    n = int(n)
    S = float(n)
    a = _normalize(a, S)
    L = _fft_len(n)
    c, A = _convolve_fft(a, L)
    best = a.copy()
    best_o = _score_from_c(c, n)

    if time.monotonic() >= float(deadline):
        return best, best_o

    start = time.monotonic()
    last_print = start
    gap = max(float(gap_min), float(gap0))
    lr = float(lr0)
    beta = float(momentum)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    print(f"hard {tag} n={n} start={best_o:.6f} log={bool(log_mode)}")

    while time.monotonic() < min(float(deadline), start + 10**9):
        it += 1
        old_o = _score_from_c(c, n)

        if old_o < best_o - 1e-10:
            best_o = old_o
            best = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            if time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:
                print(f"  hard {tag} it={it} obj={best_o:.6f} "
                      f"gap={gap:.6f} lr={lr:.5f}")
                last_print = time.monotonic()
        else:
            stall += 1

        p, _ = _top_weights(c, gap)
        g = _grad_fft(a, L, A, p)

        if log_mode:
            Scur = float(a.sum())
            if Scur <= 1e-300:
                Scur = float(n)
            mu = float(np.sum(a * g) / Scur)
            d = a * (g - mu)
        else:
            d = g - float(np.mean(g))

        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
        if scale < 1e-15:
            if stall > 800:
                break
            a = _normalize(a * (1.0 + 1e-6 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)), S)
            c, A = _convolve_fft(a, L)
            v.fill(0.0)
            stall = 0
            continue

        v = beta * v - lr * (d / scale)
        maxv = float(np.max(np.abs(v)))
        if maxv > 0.25:
            v *= 0.25 / maxv

        if log_mode:
            loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
            loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
            loga = loga + v
            loga = loga - float(np.max(loga))
            e = np.exp(loga)
            s = float(e.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                a = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                a = e * (S / s)
        else:
            a_new = a + v
            a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
            s = float(a_new.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
                a = np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                a = a_new * (S / s)

        c_new, A_new = _convolve_fft(a, L)
        o_new = _score_from_c(c_new, n)
        tol = 1e-10 + 0.003 * old_o

        if np.isfinite(o_new) and o_new <= old_o + tol:
            c = c_new
            A = A_new
            if o_new < old_o - 1e-10:
                lr = min(lr * 1.04, lr_max)
            else:
                lr = max(lr * 0.98, lr0 * 0.4)
        else:
            v = v * 0.2
            lr = max(lr * 0.5, 1e-7)
            beta = max(0.0, beta * 0.95)

        elapsed = max(1e-6, time.monotonic() - start)
        gap = max(float(gap_min), float(gap0) * float(np.exp(-elapsed / max(1.0, float(anneal_s)))))

        if stall > 3000:
            mode = int(rng.integers(0, 2))
            if mode == 0:
                w = int(rng.integers(2, max(3, n // 25)))
                s0 = int(rng.integers(0, n - w))
                a[s0:s0 + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.10, size=w))
            else:
                f1 = float(rng.uniform(1, 4))
                ph = float(rng.uniform(0, 2 * np.pi))
                yy = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
                a *= np.exp(0.08 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * yy + ph))
            a = _normalize(a, S)
            c, A = _convolve_fft(a, L)
            v.fill(0.0)
            stall = 0

    return best, float(best_o)


def _quick_refine(a, n, seconds, tag, rng):
    n = int(n)
    a = _normalize(a, float(n))
    rem = max(1.0, float(seconds))
    end = time.monotonic() + rem
    nL = _fft_len(n)

    if end - time.monotonic() <= 3.0:
        c, _ = _convolve_fft(a, nL)
        return a, _score_from_c(c, n)

    end1 = time.monotonic() + 0.65 * rem
    a, o = _smooth_run(
        a, n, end1, tag + "s", rng,
        tau0_frac=0.30, tau_min_frac=1e-5,
        lr0=0.10, log_mode=True, momentum=0.5,
        anneal_s=max(3.0, 0.20 * rem)
    )

    if time.monotonic() < end:
        a, o = _hard_run(
            a, n, end, tag + "h", rng,
            lr0=0.05, log_mode=False, momentum=0.4,
            gap0=0.04, gap_min=1e-5,
            anneal_s=max(3.0, 0.20 * rem)
        )

    return a, float(o)


def _run_refine(a, n, end, tag, rng):
    n = int(n)
    a = _normalize(a, float(n))
    now = time.monotonic()
    rem = max(0.0, float(end) - now)

    if rem <= 5.0:
        c, _ = _convolve_fft(a, _fft_len(n))
        return a, _score_from_c(c, n)

    end1 = now + 0.70 * rem
    a, o = _smooth_run(
        a, n, end1, tag + "s", rng,
        tau0_frac=0.50, tau_min_frac=1e-5,
        lr0=0.12, log_mode=True, momentum=0.6,
        anneal_s=max(10.0, 0.25 * rem),
        flat_alpha=0.0
    )

    if time.monotonic() < float(end):
        a, o = _hard_run(
            a, n, float(end), tag + "h", rng,
            lr0=0.06, log_mode=False, momentum=0.4,
            gap0=0.05, gap_min=1e-5,
            anneal_s=max(10.0, 0.25 * rem)
        )

    return a, float(o)


def _perturb_sequence(a, n, rng, mode=0, sigma=0.20):
    n = int(n)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if n <= 1:
        return a
    b = a.copy()
    S = float(b.sum())

    if mode == 0:
        w = int(rng.integers(2, max(3, n // 18)))
        if w >= n:
            w = max(2, n // 10)
        s = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        b[s:s + w] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(sigma), size=w))
    elif mode == 1:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        f1 = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 4.0))
        f2 = float(rng.uniform(4.0, 12.0))
        p1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        mod = 0.7 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f1 * x + p1) + 0.3 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f2 * x + p2)
        b *= np.exp(float(sigma) * mod)
    else:
        w = max(2, n // 50)
        if w >= n:
            w = max(2, n // 10)
        s1 = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        s2 = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))
        if s1 == s2:
            s2 = (s1 + w // 2) % max(1, n - w + 1)
        tmp = b[s1:s1 + w].copy()
        b[s1:s1 + w] = b[s2:s2 + w]
        b[s2:s2 + w] = tmp

    b = np.maximum(b, 0.0)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(n, S / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return b * (S / s)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    seed = int(seed)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    start = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = start + max(60.0, budget - 20.0)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim == 1 and p.size > 0:
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            if p.sum() > 1e-12:
                parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_n = 4096
    best_o = np.inf

    n_candidates = []
    if parent is not None and 1000 <= int(parent.size) <= 10000:
        n_candidates.append(int(parent.size))
    n_candidates.extend([4096, 8192])

    seen = set()
    n_clean = []
    for nn in n_candidates:
        nn = int(nn)
        if nn > 0 and nn not in seen:
            seen.add(nn)
            n_clean.append(nn)

    param_cands = []

    print(f"start parametric scouting, parent={'yes' if parent is not None else 'no'}")
    for nn in n_clean:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 260.0:
            break
        t0 = time.monotonic()
        a, o = _best_parameterized_init(nn, rng, parent)
        dt = time.monotonic() - t0
        print(f"param n={nn} obj={o:.6f} time={dt:.1f}s")
        if a is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(a)) and o < np.inf:
            param_cands.append((float(o), len(param_cands), a, int(nn)))
            if o < best_o:
                best_o = float(o)
                best_n = int(nn)
                best_a = a.copy()

    if param_cands:
        param_cands.sort()

    if best_a is None:
        best_n = 4096
        best_a = _generate_initial(0, best_n, rng, parent)
        c, _ = _convolve_fft(best_a, _fft_len(best_n))
        best_o = _score_from_c(c, best_n)
        param_cands.append((float(best_o), 0, best_a, int(best_n)))

    # Refine a couple of the best parameterized seeds before the main polish.
    for o, idx, a, nn in param_cands[:2]:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 240.0:
            break
        alloc = min(150.0, max(30.0, 0.08 * budget))
        end = min(deadline - 220.0, now + alloc)
        if end <= now + 4.0:
            continue
        a2, o2 = _run_refine(a, nn, end, f"seed{idx}", rng)
        print(f"seed refine idx={idx} n={nn} obj={o2:.6f}")
        if o2 < best_o and a2 is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(a2)):
            best_o = float(o2)
            best_n = int(nn)
            best_a = a2.copy()

    # Long final refinement on the current best.
    rem = max(1.0, deadline - 10.0 - time.monotonic())
    phase = max(120.0, 0.35 * rem)
    end1 = min(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + phase)
    if end1 > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        best_a, best_o = _smooth_run(
            best_a, best_n, end1, "final_smooth", rng,
            tau0_frac=0.45, tau_min_frac=1e-6,
            lr0=0.12, lr_max=0.25,
            log_mode=True, momentum=0.6,
            anneal_s=max(20.0, 0.25 * phase),
            flat_alpha=0.0
        )

    rem = max(1.0, deadline - 10.0 - time.monotonic())
    phase = max(60.0, 0.15 * rem)
    end2 = min(deadline - 5.0, time.monotonic() + phase)
    if end2 > time.monotonic() + 5.0:
        best_a, best_o = _hard_run(
            best_a, best_n, end2, "final_hard", rng,
            lr0=0.06, log_mode=False, momentum=0.4,
            gap0=0.035, gap_min=1e-6,
            anneal_s=max(10.0, 0.20 * phase)
        )

    # Perturb + quick refine loop.
    attempt = 0
    no_improve = 0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
        attempt += 1
        if attempt <= 5:
            sigma = 0.14
        elif attempt <= 15:
            sigma = 0.24
        else:
            sigma = min(0.50, 0.14 + 0.02 * float(attempt))

        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3))
        if mode == 2 and attempt % 4 != 0:
            mode = 0

        a_p = _perturb_sequence(best_a, best_n, rng, mode=mode, sigma=sigma)
        remain = deadline - 25.0 - time.monotonic()
        if remain <= 8.0:
            break
        qsec = min(30.0, max(8.0, 0.055 * remain))
        a_q, o_q = _quick_refine(a_p, best_n, qsec, f"pert{attempt}", rng)

        if o_q < best_o - 1e-10 and a_q is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(a_q)):
            best_o = float(o_q)
            best_a = a_q.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            print(f"perturb {attempt} n={best_n} obj={best_o:.6f}")
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        # Occasional fresh restarts, possibly with a different length.
        if no_improve > 0 and no_improve % 12 == 0 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 140.0:
            fresh_n = 4096 if ((no_improve // 12) % 2 == 0) else 8192
            a_f, o_f = _best_parameterized_init(fresh_n, rng, parent)
            qsec2 = min(60.0, max(20.0, 0.04 * (deadline - 140.0 - time.monotonic())))
            a_f, o_f = _quick_refine(a_f, fresh_n, qsec2, f"fresh{no_improve}", rng)
            if o_f < best_o - 1e-10 and a_f is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(a_f)):
                best_o = float(o_f)
                best_n = int(fresh_n)
                best_a = a_f.copy()
                no_improve = 0
                print(f"fresh restart n={best_n} obj={best_o:.6f}")

    # Final short polish.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        best_a, best_o = _hard_run(
            best_a, best_n, deadline, "tiny_polish", rng,
            lr0=0.03, log_mode=False, momentum=0.3,
            gap0=0.012, gap_min=1e-6, anneal_s=4.0
        )

    # Scale to a safe sum. The objective is scale-invariant, so this does not
    # change the mathematical score, and keeps every entry below the evaluator cap.
    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        out = np.full(max(1, int(best_n)), 1000.0 / float(max(1, int(best_n))), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = out * (1000.0 / s)
    out = np.minimum(out, 1000.0)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)

    result = [float(x) for x in out]

    try:
        final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
    except Exception:
        final_score = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(final_score):
        # Extremely safe fallback.
        result = [1000.0 / float(max(1, int(best_n)))] * int(max(1, int(best_n)))
        try:
            final_score = float(evaluate_sequence(result))
        except Exception:
            final_score = np.inf

    print(f"FINAL internal_obj={best_o:.6f} evaluator_score={final_score:.6f} n={best_n}")
    return result
```
